THE BUTTERFLY EFFECT

The machine stops screaming before you do.

You sit strapped in, sweating, your hands shaking on the armrests. It was a systems test. Nothing was
supposed to go anywhere. The panels are dark and the controls are dead.

You open the hatch expecting the concrete floor of the test bay. You get dirt. A mule cart rattles
past close enough to touch, and the driver shouts at you in Latin. You understand him.

Thank God I took that in college.

Ancient Rome. Not ruins. Alive.

The machine is dead weight. Its contacts and couplings are gold, and gold you can carry: you pry out
60 aurei worth of it. Every piece will have to go back before the machine could ever run again.
Rome pays in silver denarii. Gold has to be changed at the money changers (exchange <n> aurei).

Two things on this street could use your money, and you can afford only one:
  choose fountain  — The Subura's fountain runs brown. Clear the lead pipe and reline the basin.
  choose workshop  — Pay for a second forge and paid apprentices at the smith's by the Porta Trigemina, in exchange for a share of the business.
Your ears are still ringing. Before anything else you could walk and get your bearings: visit market, changers, forges, curia or subura (no Attention).
Type 'help' for commands.

  • At the small baths behind the Circus Maximus you sit in the warm room between a fuller and a man selling olives. The olive man complains that a merchant under the Aventine pays porters to bail his cellar every morning, and still the jars go green. The fuller says the merchant should sacrifice to the river and stop complaining.

== Month 243 · Martius, AD 175 ==  21,790 denarii (+41/month) · 34 aurei (1 = 33.7 den.)   Index 115.2

  The Club (Titus Flavius Rufus): you hold 100% (control), strength 97 (50.5% of Governance), loyalty 90, chartered
  ► Cassianus: commission look cellarpump (unpaid)   (commission)
  Machine: 9/9 repair steps, gold 60/60 aurei — ready to jump   (machine)
  ► The era's 20 years are over. Jump when you're ready (you can also stay).

> jump
You will leave AD 175 and arrive somewhere between AD 210 and AD 225: the machine's range is 35–50 years (repairs, 0 of 3 upgrades, and your time in this era). You can't come back.
What you leave behind (21,790 denarii and 34 aurei in hand):
  • No last orders for the Club (leading now: the Aventine Reformers): orders club reform|clientele [1|2 to name who succeeds you: 1 Titus Flavius Rufus, a retired centurion who likes order more than reform; 2 Gnaea Seia, a fuller's widow who runs the street association and trusts no senator].
  • The Club of the Aventine would be left bare (chartered but not endowed, so it counts as bare), losing 10% of its strength each decade (now 97).
  •   It holds no money, so it can't pay down Governance debt while you're away. (endow club <denarii>)
  • The machine will carry you 35–50 years (repairs, 0 of 3 upgrades, and your time in this era); exactly how far, you'll know when you arrive. Upgrades and more time here would lengthen it.
  • The 21,790 denarii in your hands stay behind and are lost unless you spend them, pay down debt or endow an institution.
  • Your gold: the machine can carry 10 aurei; you hold 34 aurei, so 24 aurei would be left behind and lost (deposit <n> or bury <n>, 1 Attention each; the bank takes up to 100 aurei, a jar holds 50 aurei).
  •   Denarii can't be carried, banked or buried usefully: change them into gold first (exchange <n> denarii).
Prepare: paydown <domain> <points> · endow <inst> <denarii|all> · audit <inst> · exchange <n> denarii · deposit <aurei> · bury <aurei>. Type 'jump' again to go, or anything else to stay.

> audit club
The Club has an audit charter (1,000 denarii, 3 Attention).  [Attention left this month: 0/4]
What you leave behind (20,790 denarii and 34 aurei in hand):
  • No last orders for the Club (leading now: the Aventine Reformers): orders club reform|clientele [1|2 to name who succeeds you: 1 Titus Flavius Rufus, a retired centurion who likes order more than reform; 2 Gnaea Seia, a fuller's widow who runs the street association and trusts no senator].
  • The Club of the Aventine would be left bare (chartered but not endowed, so it counts as bare), losing 10% of its strength each decade (now 97).
  •   It holds no money, so it can't pay down Governance debt while you're away. (endow club <denarii>)
  • The machine will carry you 35–50 years (repairs, 0 of 3 upgrades, and your time in this era); exactly how far, you'll know when you arrive. Upgrades and more time here would lengthen it.
  • The 20,790 denarii in your hands stay behind and are lost unless you spend them, pay down debt or endow an institution.
  • Your gold: the machine can carry 10 aurei; you hold 34 aurei, so 24 aurei would be left behind and lost (deposit <n> or bury <n>, 1 Attention each; the bank takes up to 100 aurei, a jar holds 50 aurei).
  •   Denarii can't be carried, banked or buried usefully: change them into gold first (exchange <n> denarii).
Prepare: paydown <domain> <points> · endow <inst> <denarii|all> · audit <inst> · exchange <n> denarii · deposit <aurei> · bury <aurei>. Type 'jump' again to go, or anything else to stay.
  (No Attention left this month. Type 'end' to end the month.)

> endow club all
That needs 2 Attention; you have 0 left this month.

> jump

The machine shudders. Decades pass in the dark... It carries you 50 years.

— Recognition —
You climb out of the machine in AD 225. The Tiber smells the same. Near the Porta Trigemina you find
the smith's workshop you funded by its soot-black wall. It is a stable now. Down the street, the
fountain you didn't choose was filled in long ago. An old woman calls the spot 'fever corner'.

— Wrongness —
Something is wrong. Magistrates post their rulings in the forum and people stop to read them. Nobody
seems afraid of the soldiers. The coins are lighter and paler than you remember, barely half silver
now. Prices have climbed with every cut, and soldiers grumble that their pay buys less each year.

— Personal echo —
Demetria's letters survive in a library copy. They name everyone who stayed to help the sick in 166.
You are not among them; she never thought to ask you. Over the door of the Club of the Aventine
someone has painted its beginning in red letters: 'Begun by a stranger from the East.'

— Discovery —
The Club of the Aventine, which you founded, is still there and still itself. Its head is a successor
of Titus Flavius Rufus. In your purse: the 10 aurei you carried, as good as the day you left. The
gold you couldn't carry, and didn't hide or bank (24 aurei), is long gone.

You can walk around Rome: visit market, changers, forges, curia or subura (no Attention).
Type 'learn more' for the Index and what became of your institutions, 'jump' to go on another 25–40 years, or 'quit'.

Crises while you were away
  AD 189: the pestilence returns to Rome under Commodus. At its worst two thousand die in a day; the emperor goes to Laurentum for the clean air. It is contained: about 19 thousand dead, 2% of Rome.


(after arrival) > why medicine
You have been away for 50 years. What happened in between is lost to you; the world simply is what it is now. (Try 'learn more'.)

(after arrival) > quit
